HFT visibility is governed by the authoring policy above.","docket_mode":"quarantine_without_hft","docket_ready":true}},"selected_context_units":[],"support_registry":[{"branch_refs":["root_000266/B001","root_000266/B003","root_000266/B011","root_001525/B001","root_001525/B002"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-2","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_09de73964abb7611c9cd","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"31:8:7\",\"root_000266\",[\"B001\",\"B003\",\"B011\"]],[\"31:8:8\",\"root_001525\",[\"B001\",\"B002\"]]],\"grade\":\"strong\",\"text\":\"Bahçeler, onarıcı işin karşısına rastgele konmuş bir ödül değil, büyüyen, örten ve koruyan bir esenlik çevresi olarak belirir.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":["root_000054/B002"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:1","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_1d47c87c97576249dcdf","text":"1. **Trust made executable as sound action.** Lexical evidence: `ء م ن B002` is trusting assent and confirmation, `ع م ل B001` is intentional doing, and `ص ل ح B001` is soundness opposed to corruption. Structural cue: faith and work are joined as coordinated completed actions; 31:4 gives their practical grammar, while 31:6-7 show speech and hearing detached from sound response. Assigned role: assent establishes trust, work externalizes it, and soundness qualifies its effect. Abductive move (inferred): confirmation that never crosses into intentional action remains structurally incomplete in this sequence. Reading change: faith reads as a trust that becomes publicly testable through repair-producing work.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000266/B003","root_001525/B001"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:2","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_47228d3bc5d56fd152ad","text":"2. **The garden as the ecological form of soundness.** Lexical evidence: `ج ن ن B003` is a tree-covered garden, with `ج ن ن B001` contributing protective covering; `ن ع م B001` is pleasant well-being and bestowed favor. Structural cue: 31:10 soon narrates rain, germination, and varied noble growth, while 31:12 frames benefit through gratitude. Assigned role: garden cover materializes the abundant, sheltered condition toward which sound acts point. Abductive move (inferred): the later growth chain activates an ecological analogy between moral repair and a flourishing habitat. Reading change: `جَنَّٰتُ ٱلنَّعِيمِ` becomes the fitting regenerative environment of repaired action, not an arbitrary prize attached to it.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":[],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:retrospective_surprises_md:1","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_618a9e6b5dc673d66a4d","text":"Ayah 31:10 supplied the rain-growth ecology behind the covered garden, and 31:32 showed recognized favor surviving rescue only through fidelity. These later scenes made well-being an actively sustained relation rather than a passive reward-space.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000266/B003","root_001525/B001"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:2","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_67db33969e9941fe4455","text":"2. **Covered growth as the material form of ease.** **ج ن ن B003** is a tree-covered garden, supported by **B001** covering and **B011** vigorous growth; **ن ع م B001–B002** supplies bestowed well-being and soft ease. The later rain-and-growth scene provides a material relation: covering vegetation is produced abundance that in turn shelters. The causal arrow from cultivation to protected ease is abductive. The gardens thus read as a developed ecology of well-being, not merely a location.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000054/B001","root_000876/B001","root_001046/B001"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:1","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_7cc6636ff65775324ca8","text":"1. **Trust externalized as reparative work.** **ء م ن B001–B002** supplies settled trust and trusting assent; **ع م ل B001** intentional work; **ص ل ح B001** soundness against corruption and **B002** reconciliation. The coordination of believing and doing makes work the outward operation of security. I infer that trusted order is enacted by repairing what is unsound.
